After the transition to democracy, Bulgaria's current prime minister apologized to the Turks and condemned the process. After 30 years of forced migration, many problems such as the Bulgarian Turks' residence permit, work permit, citizenship, social security still await a solution. As it is known, the social security problems of our migrant citizens from Bulgaria have always been the first problem ever since the 1970s. The transfer of social rights, envisioned by Article 12 of the 1968 Immigration Agreement, has failed to be carried out due to the Bulgarian side's stance. According to Law No. 5574, affected by forced migration, our relatives have borrowed money from the foreign currency base and have retired around 36,000 of our citizens throughout Turkey, but according to Law 403, about 2,000 of our descendants, who came in as a continuation of the mandatory migration, have caused a major injustice. Even though they work in Turkey, people who have not been insured or are late and have reached 70 are unable to retire today. In Articles 5 and 6 of the proposal, the Central Bank has changed its provisions concerning the dissolution of its annual profits and has been harmonised by provisions in the Turkish Trade Law. According to the 60th article of the Central Bank Law, 20 per cent of the profits are earmarked and 6 per cent of the stocks are split into shareholders as the first profit share, and 5 per cent of the remaining amount is divided into shareholders, not to exceed two months' salary, 10 per cent to the bank members, and 6 per cent of the shareholders are earmarked as the second profit share over the nominal value of the stocks. After this distribution, the profits will be taken over to the Treasury. The 519th article of the Turkish Trade Law, issued in 2011, envisions that 5 per cent of the annual profits be earmarked as a reserve amount until they reach 20 per cent of the paid capital, and that some bonuses, expenses and responses are reduced, and that 10 per cent of the remaining part will be split as a reserve. Thus, the National Bank's annual profits are divided from the Turkish Trade Law at a higher rate than predicted and without limited capital, its reserve acoustics are constantly growing. Under the arrangement, the first order, which will be a very low amount of $5,000, given the central bank's $25,000 capital, is expected to remain intact at all times, and the second set of backups will be separated by 10 per cent in line with the Turkish Trade Law. It consists of two major chapters, including balance sheet, active and passive, such as assets and resources, which are also studied at commercial high schools and universities in education. Beings and resources must be equal. It's called a balance sheet basic equation. On the balance sheet, every being has a source. Because a being has to be a source to be taken. It could be resources, or debt, or foreign resources. Therefore, there is no basis for claiming that the benchmarks found among the central bank's original sources are not in return for the balance sheet and that money will be printed to transfer from here to the Treasury, which is a total lie. Mr. President, good lawmakers, as you know, in the first quarter of 2018, the ratings agencies, who have expressed their intentions with their rhetoric and evaluations and who have started to show their goals with their grade declines after the second quarter, have increased their pressure on the courtship when they fail to achieve their initial attacks, and they aim to impose on Turkey's economic difficulties by failing to fund their grades. However, our real and financial sector met its obligations without any difficulty between August and September 2018, when the perception of Turkey was very poor. Over the past year, $50 billion in savings has been recorded, a $10 billion reduction in our country's foreign debt supply, and a $30 billion reduction in companies' debt in foreign currency. However, credit rating agencies continue to reduce our rating. What is this attitude, if not political? The figures I've just expressed clearly show that even though the ratios of Turkey's economy are better than the equivalent countries, our willingness to enter into turbulence since the summer of 2018 clearly reveals the magnitude of our attacks. As our President has made clear, those who carried out these attacks have not succeeded. This clearly shows the power of the Turkish economy and where we're coming from. Turkey's economy has started to accelerate upwardly towards goals in the New Economy Programme, inflation has taken a trend in decline, tourism revenues have increased substantially, and the Central Bank's reserves have increased. An effective work has been created, initially by the co-ordination of our Treasury and Finance Ministry and by the time the relevant agencies intervene in time, and these attacks have been eliminated. Our proposal for the law in this scope aims to eliminate the negative effects caused by economic attacks, and to continue sustainable growth despite all global and regional challenges. The content of our law proposal is edited for the restructuring of troubled loans due to an increase in troubled loans in banks. It should not be forgotten that in a country where problem loans are quickly and effectively resolved, credit supply is high and credit costs are low. Banks aim to narrow down credit supply, invest their money in short-term and liquid assets, so that they can meet capital competence and liquidity rates, which are set by banking authority. The bank sector's balance sheets confirm that from September 2018 to May 2019, there was no nominal increase in bank loans. Mr. President, valued lawmakers are being targeted to boost the active quality of banks through financial restructurings to be implemented. With financial restructuring, the fact that firms will bring value to reinvestment will improve the balance sheets of both banks and companies, so production, investment, export, trade, employment, in short, is the most important element of sustainable growth. Some tax exceptions, such as bank and insurance treatment tax, added value tax, stamp tax, Supporting Resources Fund, are also encouraged to restructure. In this framework, with the 16th and 17th amendments of the proposed law, the banks are entitled to two years of financial restructuring; the President is authorized to extend that time for another two years. This practice is largely similar to the restoration process, which has been done in the past and is widely known as the Istanbul approach. However, another important issue involved in the proposal is the specific responses. As you know, regular responses are used as a means of monetary policy to help reduce potential risks in the banking sector. In this framework, we organise the consideration of changes in the domestic or external pencils of financial institutions in the setting of regular reciprocal response rates, including the changing market conditions mentioned in our proposal. Another important arrangement that stands out in our proposal is the transfer of the depositories to the Treasury at the Turkish Central Bank of the Republic. A number of problems arise because the Federal Reserve, which is not expected to make a profit as a rule, has been the most profitable corporation in our country in recent years. The Central Bank does not need to allocate much of its profits as a requirement, as there is no financial hardship involved. Any profit that is not transferred to the treasury provides an additional amount of debt as much as the non-distributed profit, causing domestic debt rates to remain equally artificially high. On the other hand, the profit of the Turkish Central Bank is the money that was withdrawn from the market in the final analysis. The higher the profits, the greater the need to fund the market. If you look at world examples, it appears that they've transferred the entire or almost all of the profits of the central banks to the treasury. For example, the central banks of Canada, Mexico and China transfer 100 per cent of their profits to the treasury. Poland transfers the profits to the treasury at 98 per cent of the profits. As a result, the world's central banks transfer an average of 85 per cent of their profits to the treasury, while Turkey's rate is 67 per cent. From this point of view, it is also necessary to say that as a subject that has been very busy with the public's agenda in recent days, the resuscitation does not mean the printing of money by the Turkish Republic's Central Bank. This perception is a false perception. Our proposal is to ensure that the provision of the reserve of the Central Bank Law and the distribution of profits will be in harmony with the world's examples and with the Turkish Trade Law. On the other hand, it is also provided that information requested by relevant financial institutions and organisations to be met in real time and momentum so that the Central Bank can make a quick and effective decision. With all these arrangements, to sum up our proposal in general: In order to simplify tax practices, to reduce taxpayers' tax harmonisation costs, 10 per cent of non-profit revenues are based on tax-related gains for taxpayers who are involved in domestic passenger transport and receive all their revenues by electronic collection system. The duration of registration of money, gold, foreign currency, securities and other capital markets abroad is extended until 31 December 2019 and taxing by 1 per cent of all of them, Expedition of non-existence fees from 15 TL to 50 TL, The current arrangements for the monthly insurance holders, which are tied to foreign loans, are due to a negative impact on the financial structure of our Social Security institution, as a result of the fact that these people have repaid the amount they owe in a very short period of time as a pension, as well as that the balance of benefits and burdens for the institution and persons is not appropriate, raising the debt rate from 32% to 45 percent. Tax support for the production of electric-engine automotive brands or brands in our country and the preferential use of these vehicles in the country. In violation of customs regulations, the removal of these victims of third-intentioned victims in connection with vehicles that were brought in from abroad and sold to good third parties, Health, energy investments, electronic telecommunications, and also changes in our proposal to implement legal regulations needed to address the problems used by newly established universities for the moving-ins. As I cease to speak with these feelings and thoughts, I wish our General Assembly's law proposal talks to our country, our nation, good luck and good fortune.

With these two articles, the changes are being made to the banks' mandatory response to the Central Bank. In this way, banks are meant to provide additional loans to the market by providing an ease in terms of capital. In the current application, banks have to make mandatory responses for pencils, such as the deposits they collect on the sidelines of their balance sheets, the loans they use. In the arrangement we have discussed, the Central Bank will be able to ask the banks to allocate a mandatory response for all of its markers inside and out of the balance sheet. So the Central Bank is going to ask the banks to make a mandatory response to their active pens. For example, the option that banks have already used is that derivatives such as swap, futures, are kept out of the balance sheet. But their profits from these transactions are shown in the balance sheet. When the regulation is enforced, the derivative of this type will be asked for in return. According to the arrangement, banks will also account for loans and derivative vehicles in their assets for mandatory responses at the Central Bank. New decisions are being made in our crisis to save the future of financial capital, to sustain its insatiable flow and production of value. Second, taxing the poor to pay the bail of the capital's own crisis by paying the debt to the poor and controlling the future of the poor through the debts that he has approved, or modern slavery.
